 <div className="flex mb-3">
                      {[...Array(reviews[current].rating)].map((_, i) => (
                        <Star key={i} className="w-4 h-4 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed text-pretty">
                      "{reviews[current].text}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </motion.div>
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-4">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCurrent(c => (c - 1 + reviews.length) % reviews.length)}
          className="p-2 rounded-full bg-muted hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
        >
          <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
          {reviews.map((_, i) => (
            <button
              key={i}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setCurrent(i)}
              className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${i === current ? 'w-6 bg-primary' : 'w-1.5 bg-muted-foreground/30'}`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setCurrent(c => (c + 1) % reviews.length)}
          className="p-2 rounded-full bg-muted hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
        >
          <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* 无限滚动行 */
function MarqueeRow({ items, direction = 'left', speed = 28 }: {
  items: typeof reviews;
  direction?: 'left' | 'right';
  speed?: number;
}) {
  const doubled = [...items, ...items];
  const pausedRef = useRef(false);
  const posRef = useRef(0);
  const rafRef = useRef(0);
  const rowRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const CARD_W = 304; // 288px card + 2*8px mx

  useEffect(() => {
    const totalW = items.length * CARD_W;
    const step = direction === 'left' ? -speed / 60 : speed / 60;
    const animate = () => {
      if (!pausedRef.current) {
        posRef.current += step;
        if (posRef.current <= -totalW) posRef.current += totalW;
        if (posRef.current >= 0) posRef.current -= totalW;
        if (rowRef.current) rowRef.current.style.transform = `translateX(${posRef.current}px)`;
      }
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
  }, [direction, speed, items.length]);

  return (
    <div
      className="overflow-hidden"
      onMouseEnter={() => { pausedRef.current = true; }}
      onMouseLeave={() => { pausedRef.current = false; }}
    >
      <div ref={rowRef} className="flex" style={{ willChange: 'transform' }}>
        {doubled.map((r, i) => <ReviewCard key={i} review={r} />)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ReviewCarousel() {
  const row1 = reviews.slice(0, Math.ceil(reviews.length / 2));
  const row2 = reviews.slice(Math.ceil(reviews.length / 2));
  // 补足偶数以视觉均衡
  const fill = reviews[0];
  const r2 = row2.length < 3 ? [...row2, fill, fill] : row2;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 overflow-hidden">
      <MarqueeRow items={row1} direction="left" speed={26} />
      <MarqueeRow items={r2} direction="right" speed={22} />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ─────────────────────── Three.js 3D Hero 背景 ─────────────────────── */
function HeroBackground() {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;

    // ── 场景、相机、渲染器 ──
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 200);
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 28);

    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
    renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
    renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;
    mount.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

    // ── 主题色 ──
    const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');
    const PRIMARY_H = 162;
    const primaryColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H}, 63%, 45%)`);
    const accentColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H + 40}, 70%, 55%)`);
    const deepColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(220, 70%, 55%)`);

    // ── 雾气 ──
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(isDark ? 0x040b10 : 0xeef7f4, 0.018);
    if (isDark) scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x040b10);
    else scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xeef7f4);

    // ── 环境光 + 方向光 ──
    const ambLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(primaryColor, isDark ? 0.4 : 0.7);
    scene.add(ambLight);

    const dirLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(primaryColor, isDark ? 1.8 : 1.2);
    dirLight1.position.set(10, 15, 10);
    scene.add(dirLight1);

    const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(deepColor, isDark ? 1.0 : 0.6);
    dirLight2.position.set(-10, -8, 5);
    scene.add(dirLight2);

    const pointLight = new THREE.PointLight(accentColor, isDark ? 2.5 : 1.5, 40);
    pointLight.position.set(0, 0, 15);
    scene.add(pointLight);

    // ── 浮动粒子球群 ──
    const particleCount = 1800;
    const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
    const pColors = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
    const sizes = new Float32Array(particleCount);
    const colorOptions = [primaryColor, accentColor, deepColor,
      new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H + 20}, 80%, 65%)`),
      new THREE.Color(`hsl(200, 80%, 60%)`),
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
      const r = 18 + Math.random() * 20;
      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
      positions[i * 3]     = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
      positions[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
      positions[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.cos(phi);
      const c = colorOptions[Math.floor(Math.random() * colorOptions.length)];
      pColors[i * 3]     = c.r;
      pColors[i * 3 + 1] = c.g;
      pColors[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;
      sizes[i] = Math.random() * 2.5 + 0.5;
    }
    particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
    particleGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pColors, 3));
    particleGeo.setAttribute('size', new THREE.BufferAttribute(sizes, 1));

    const particleMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      vertexShader: `
        attribute float size;
        attribute vec3 color;
        varying vec3 vColor;
        varying float vAlpha;
        void main() {
          vColor = color;
          vec4 mvPos = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
          float depth = (-mvPos.z - 10.0) / 50.0;
          vAlpha = clamp(1.0 - depth, 0.15, 0.9);
          gl_PointSize = size * (300.0 / -mvPos.z);
          gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
        }
      `,
      fragmentShader: `
        varying vec3 vColor;
        varying float vAlpha;
        void main() {
          float d = length(gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5));
          if (d > 0.5) discard;
          float alpha = smoothstep(0.5, 0.0, d) * vAlpha;
          gl_FragColor = vec4(vColor, alpha);
        }
      `,
      transparent: true,
      depthWrite: false,
      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
      vertexColors: true,
    });
    const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat);
    scene.add(particles);

    // ── 中心能量球 ──
    const coreGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.5, 4);
    const coreMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
      color: primaryColor,
      metalness: 0.3,
      roughness: 0.1,
      transmission: 0.6,
      thickness: 1.5,
      envMapIntensity: 2.0,
      emissive: primaryColor,
      emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.3 : 0.15,
    });
    const core = new THREE.Mesh(coreGeo, coreMat);
    scene.add(core);

    // ── 外层线框球 ──
    const wireGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(4.6, 2);
    const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color: primaryColor,
      wireframe: true,
      transparent: true,
      opacity: isDark ? 0.12 : 0.08,
    });
    const wireSphere = new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat);
    scene.add(wireSphere);

    // ── 轨道环 ──
    const rings: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
    const ringConfigs = [
      { r: 7.5, tube: 0.03, color: primaryColor, opacity: isDark ? 0.5 : 0.35, rotX: Math.PI / 3, rotY: 0 },
      { r: 10, tube: 0.025, color: accentColor, opacity: isDark ? 0.35 : 0.25, rotX: Math.PI / 5, rotY: Math.PI / 4 },
      { r: 13, tube: 0.02, color: deepColor, opacity: isDark ? 0.25 : 0.18, rotX: -Math.PI / 4, rotY: Math.PI / 6 },
    ];
    ringConfigs.forEach(cfg => {
      const geo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(cfg.r, cfg.tube, 16, 120);
      const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: cfg.color, transparent: true, opacity: cfg.opacity,
      });
      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
      mesh.rotation.x = cfg.rotX;
      mesh.rotation.y = cfg.rotY;
      rings.push(mesh);
      scene.add(mesh);
    });

    // ── 轨道上的小球节点 ──
    const orbitNodes: Array<{ mesh: THREE.Mesh; ring: number; angle: number; speed: number }> = [];
    ringConfigs.forEach((cfg, ri) => {
      const count = 4 + ri * 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.18 + Math.random() * 0.12, 8, 8);
        const mat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
          color: cfg.color,
          emissive: cfg.color,
          emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.8 : 0.5,
        });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        const angle = (i / count) * Math.PI * 2;
        orbitNodes.push({ mesh, ring: ri, angle, speed: 0.004 + Math.random() * 0.003 });
        scene.add(mesh);
      }
    });

    // ── 鼠标交互 ──
    let mouseX = 0, mouseY = 0;
    let targetRotX = 0, targetRotY = 0;
    const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      mouseX = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      mouseY = -(e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
    };
    const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      const t = e.touches[0];
      mouseX = (t.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      mouseY = -(t.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
    };
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: true });

    // ── 响应式 ──
    const onResize = () => {
      if (!mount) return;
      camera.aspect = mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);

    // ── 渲染循环 ──
    const clock = new THREE.Clock();
    let animId = 0;
    const animate = () => {
      animId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      const t = clock.getElapsedTime();

      // 鼠标跟随（惯性）
      targetRotX += (mouseY * 0.4 - targetRotX) * 0.05;
      targetRotY += (mouseX * 0.4 - targetRotY) * 0.05;

      // 中心球自转 + 鼠标响应
      core.rotation.x = t * 0.12 + targetRotX;
      core.rotation.y = t * 0.18 + targetRotY;
      core.rotation.z = t * 0.08;

      // 线框球反向缓慢旋转
      wireSphere.rotation.x = -t * 0.06 + targetRotX * 0.5;
      wireSphere.rotation.y = -t * 0.09 + targetRotY * 0.5;

      // 轨道环旋转
      rings[0].rotation.y = t * 0.22 + targetRotY;
      rings[0].rotation.z = t * 0.08;
      rings[1].rotation.x = Math.PI / 5 + t * 0.15 + targetRotX * 0.6;
      rings[1].rotation.z = t * 0.12;
      rings[2].rotation.y = -t * 0.18 + targetRotY * 0.4;
      rings[2].rotation.x = -Math.PI / 4 + t * 0.10;

      // 轨道节点运动
      orbitNodes.forEach(node => {
        node.angle += node.speed;
        const cfg = ringConfigs[node.ring];
        const ring = rings[node.ring];
        // 在轨道环的局部坐标系中计算位置
        const localPos = new THREE.Vector3(
          Math.cos(node.angle) * cfg.r,
          0,
          Math.sin(node.angle) * cfg.r,
        );
        localPos.applyEuler(ring.rotation);
        node.mesh.position.copy(localPos);
        node.mesh.scale.setScalar(0.85 + Math.sin(t * 2.5 + node.angle) * 0.15);
      });

      // 粒子整体缓慢旋转
      particles.rotation.y = t * 0.025 + targetRotY * 0.15;
      particles.rotation.x = t * 0.015 + targetRotX * 0.15;

      // 点光源脉动
      pointLight.intensity = (isDark ? 2.5 : 1.5) + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.6;
      pointLight.position.set(
        Math.sin(t * 0.5) * 8,
        Math.cos(t * 0.4) * 5,
        15 + Math.sin(t * 0.7) * 3,
      );

      renderer.render(scene, camera);
    };
    animate();

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      renderer.dispose();
      particleGeo.dispose();
      particleMat.dispose();
      coreGeo.dispose();
      coreMat.dispose();
      wireGeo.dispose();
      wireMat.dispose();
      rings.forEach(r => { r.geometry.dispose(); (r.material as THREE.Material).dispose(); });
      orbitNodes.forEach(n => { n.mesh.geometry.dispose(); (n.mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose(); });
      if (mount.contains(renderer.domElement)) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
    };
  }, []);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full" style={{ zIndex: 0 }} />;
}

function HeroBackground() {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;
    let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;

      };

        window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);

        if (mount.contains(renderer.domElement)) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
      };
    });

    return () => { cleanup?.(); };
  }, []);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full" style={{ zIndex: 0 }} />;
}

AgentThinkAction(thought="The issue is that inside `import('three').then((THREE) => {...})`, TypeScript doesn't recognize the `THREE` namespace for type annotations like `THREE.Mesh[]`. I need to either:\n1. Import the types at the top: `import type * as THREE from 'three'` - this is the cleanest approach since it's just for types and adds no runtime cost\n2. Replace type annotations with local type aliases\n\nThe cleanest fix is to add `import type * as THREE from 'three'` at the top - this gives us the types without bundling the library into the main chunk. The dynamic import handles runtime loading.", action=<ActionType.THINK: 'think'>)

网页体验、3D 数据展示、3D 产品展示、3D 建筑可视化、3D 动画、着色器特效，或任何基于浏览器的 3D 图形。\n\n## 能力概述\n\n使用 Three.js 构建浏览器端 3D 图形与交互体验的生产流水线。可创建 3D 场景、产品展示、数据可视化、游戏原型、建筑漫游、粒子特效、后期处理画面、自定义着色器——输出为可运行的 HTML/JS 或集成到现有项目中。涵盖：场景管理、几何体、PBR 材质、光照与阴影、骨骼动画、射线交互、模型加载、后处理特效、GLSL 着色器、纹理与环境贴图。\n\n## 质量标准\n\n**首次渲染即 excellence。** 首次加载时必须在视觉上令人震撼。默认光照、默认材质、没有阴影的场景是错的。\n\n**统一美学胜过功能数量。** 所有元素必须服务于统一的视觉语言——一致的色温、协调的材质粗糙度、有意义的光照层次。\n\n**性能意识贯穿始终。** 3D 渲染昂贵。始终关注 draw call、纹理内存、阴影贴图分辨率、几何体面数。在添加特效前先测量帧率。\n\n**主动创意。** 如果用户要求"一个旋转的立方体"，交付一个带有环境反射、柔和阴影、呼吸式缩放动画、以及环境雾的立方体。包含至少一个用户没要求但会欣赏的视觉细节。\n\n**内存管理是工程师的尊严。** Three.js 对象不会自动释放 GPU 内存。对不再使用的 geometry、material、texture 始终调用 `dispose()`。\n\n## 技术栈\n\n| 层级 | 工具 | 用途 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 核心 | Three.js r160+ (ES modules) | 场景图、渲染器、数学、几何体、材质 |\n| 控制器 | `three/addons/controls/` | OrbitControls、FlyControls、DragControls |\n| 加载器 | `three/addons/loaders/` | GLTF、OBJ、FBX、STL、DRACO、KTX2 |\n| 后处理 | `three/addons/postprocessing/` | EffectComposer、泛光、SSAO、景深、胶片颗粒 |\n| 数学 | `three/src/math/` | Vector3、Matrix4、Quaternion、Euler、Color |\n| 着色器 | 自定义 GLSL | ShaderMaterial、RawShaderMaterial、后期处理 ShaderPass |\n\n## 主题索引\n\n| 主题 | 参考文档 | 核心内容 |\n|------|----------|----------|\n| **基础** | `references/fundamentals.md` | Scene、Camera、Renderer、Object3D、数学工具 |\n| **几何体** | `references/geometry.md` | 内置形状、BufferGeometry、InstancedMesh、LOD |\n| **材质** | `references/materials.md` | PBR、Standard/Physical、ShaderMaterial、透明与混合 |\n| **光照** | `references/lighting.md` | 光源类型、阴影、环境贴图、IBL、三点布光 |\n| **动画** | `references/animation.md` | AnimationMixer、GLTF 动画、骨骼动画、Morph targets |\n| **交互** | `references/interaction.md` | Raycaster、控制器、鼠标/触摸/键盘输入 |\n| **加载器** | `references/loaders.md` | GLTF/GLB、OBJ/FBX/STL、加载进度、缓存、错误处理 |\n| **后处理** | `references/postprocessing.md` | EffectComposer、泛光、SSAO、景深、自定义 ShaderPass |\n| **着色器** | `references/shaders.md` | ShaderMaterial、GLSL 内置函数、常用着色器模式 |\n| **纹理** | `references/textures.md` | UV 映射、环境贴图、PBR 贴图集、RenderTarget、程序化纹理 |\n\n## 3D 生产流水线\n\n每个项目遵循相同的路径：\n\n```\n概念 → 场景搭建 → 资源加载 → 材质光照 → 动画交互 → 后期处理 → 性能优化\n```\n\n1. **概念** —— 阐明创意愿景：情绪、构图、色彩世界、独特之处\n2. **场景搭建** —— 创建 Scene、Camera、Renderer，设置渲染参数（antialias、shadowMap、colorSpace）\n3. **资源加载** —— 使用合适的 Loader 加载模型/纹理，处理加载进度与错误回退\n4. **材质光照** —— 配置 PBR 材质、设置光源（至少环境光+方向光+补光）、开启阴影\n5. **动画交互** —— 实现 AnimationMixer、OrbitControls、Raycaster 交互、响应式布局\n6. **后期处理** —— 按需添加泛光、色调映射、色彩校正\n7. **性能优化** —— InstancedMesh 合并、纹理压缩、LOD、阴影质量调优、内存 dispose\n\n## 关键实现要点\n\n### 渲染循环\n\n始终使用 `requestAnimationFrame`，在循环中更新 controls、mixer、uniforms：\n\n```javascript\nconst clock = new THREE.Clock();\n\nfunction animate() {\n  requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n  const delta = clock.getDelta();\n  controls.update();\n  mixer.update(delta);\n  composer.render();\n}\nanimate();\n```\n\n### 内存管理\n\n```javascript\n// 清理几何体\ngeometry.dispose();\n// 清理材质\nmaterial.dispose();\n// 清理纹理\ntexture.dispose();\n// 清理渲染目标\nrenderTarget.dispose();\n```\n\n### 响应式\n\n```javascript\nwindow.addEventListener("resize", () => {\n  camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\n  composer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\n});\n```\n\n### 阴影优化\n\n```javascript\nrenderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;\nrenderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;\n\ndirectionalLight.castShadow = true;\ndirectionalLight.shadow.mapSize.width = 2048;\ndirectionalLight.shadow.mapSize.height = 2048;\ndirectionalLight.shadow.camera.near = 0.5;\ndirectionalLight.shadow.camera.far = 50;\n```\n\n### ColorSpace 与 Gamma\n\n```javascript\nrenderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\nrenderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\nrenderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;\n```\n\n## 不要做\n\n- 不要在生产环境使用 `MeshBasicMaterial` 作为主体材质（不接受光照，看起来扁平）\n- 不要加载未压缩的纹理到移动端\n- 不要为每个物体单独创建相同的几何体（使用 `.clone()` 或 InstancedMesh）\n- 不要忘记在场景卸载时 dispose 所有 Three.js 对象\n- 不要在动画循环中创建新的对象（Vector3、Color 等），使用 `.set()` 复用\n\n## 参考文档索引\n\n- `references/fundamentals.md` —— Scene、Camera、Renderer 核心概念\n- `references/geometry.md` —— 所有几何体类型与高级用法\n- `references/materials.md` —— 材质系统与 PBR 工作流\n- `references/lighting.md` —— 光照、阴影、环境贴图\n- `references/animation.md` —— 动画系统与程序化动画\n- `references/interaction.md` —— 射线检测与用户输入\n- `references/loaders.md` —— 模型加载与资源管理\n- `references/postprocessing.md` —— 后期处理特效栈\n- `references/shaders.md` —— 自定义着色器开发\n- `references/textures.md` —— 纹理映射与高级纹理技术', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='threejs', success=True, data={'skill_path': SkillObservation(content='Base directory for this skill: /workspace/app-bu7wnuu44l4x/.skills/superdesign-frontend\n\n## 能力概述\n\n本技能为 Agent 提供一套结构化、现代化的前端 UI 设计方法论，帮助生成美观、可用、无障碍的网页界面。\n\n| 属性 | 值 |\n|------|-----|\n| 技能层级 | `helper-tier`（辅助层，前端开发任务时自动触发） |\n| 核心行为 | 指导 Agent 按 4 步工作流生成高质量前端 UI 代码 |\n| 工作模式 | 布局设计 → 主题设计 → 动画设计 → 代码实现 |\n| 支持平台 | Web（桌面端 + 移动端） |\n| 核心能力 | 布局规划、主题定制、微交互动画、响应式设计、无障碍支持、组件设计 |\n\n**设计工作流：**\n1. **布局设计** — 先用 ASCII 绘制页面结构草图\n2. **主题设计** — 定义颜色、字体、间距、阴影\n3. **动画设计** — 规划微交互与过渡效果\n4. **代码实现** — 生成实际 HTML/CSS/JS 代码\n\n详细规范见 `references/` 目录下各专题文档。\n\n---\n\n## 触发条件\n\n### 何时应用\n\n- 用户要求生成 UI 组件（按钮、卡片、表单、导航等）\n- 用户要求创建落地页、营销页、官网\n- 用户要求创建仪表盘、后台管理界面\n- 用户要求美化/重构现有界面\n- 用户需要前端设计建议（配色、布局、字体等）\n\n### 何时不应用\n\n- 纯后端开发任务（API、数据库、服务器配置等，无 UI 需求）\n- 用户明确要求使用特定设计系统（如 Ant Design、Element Plus）且无需通用设计指导\n- 用户只需要简单的文本排版（如 Markdown 文档），无需视觉设计\n\n---\n\n## 工作流概述\n\n### 第一步：布局设计\n\n在编写任何代码之前，先用 ASCII 格式绘制页面布局草图，明确页面结构和内容区域分布。\n\n典型页面结构示例：\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│         顶部导航栏 / 品牌标识        │\n├─────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                     │\n│            首屏 Banner              │\n│       (标题 + 行动号召按钮)         │\n│                                     │\n├─────────────────────────────────────┤\n│   功能卡片    │  功能卡片  │  功能卡片 │\n├─────────────────────────────────────┤\n│              页脚                    │\n└─────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 第二步：主题设计\n\n定义统一的视觉语言：\n- **色彩**：使用 `oklch()` 定义现代色彩，避免通用 Bootstrap 蓝色（`#007bff`）\n- **字体**：从 Google Fonts 中选择（Inter、Outfit、DM Sans 等）\n- **间距**：以 0.25rem（4px）为基础单位\n- **阴影**： subtle，最多 1-2 层\n- **圆角**：现代风格 0.5-1rem，粗野风格 0px\n\n三种预设主题模式见 `references/theme-patterns.md`。\n\n### 第三步：动画设计\n\n使用微语法规划交互效果：\n\n```\n按钮按下: 150ms [缩放1→0.95→1]\n悬停上浮: 200ms [Y轴0→-2px, 阴影增强]\n淡入动画: 400ms 缓出 [Y轴+20→0, 透明度0→1]\n滑入动画: 350ms 缓出 [X轴-100→0, 透明度0→1]\n弹跳效果: 600ms [缩放0.95→1.05→1]\n```\n\n详细规范见 `references/animation-guide.md`。\n\n### 第四步：代码实现\n\n生成实际代码：\n- 原型阶段：使用 CDN 引入 Tailwind CSS、Flowbite、Lucide 图标\n- 生产阶段：使用 npm + Vite 构建（见 `references/implementation-rules.md`）\n- 图片使用 Unsplash 或 placehold.co，禁止虚构图片 URL\n- 始终遵循移动端优先的响应式设计\n\n---\n\n## 输入期望\n\n| 输入项 | 是否必填 | 说明 |\n|--------|----------|------|\n| 用户需求描述 | 是 | 要创建什么页面/组件（如"一个 SaaS 产品落地页"） |\n| 设计偏好 | 否 | 风格偏好（深色/浅色/粗野/玻璃拟态） |\n| 目标平台 | 否 | 桌面端优先或移动端优先（默认移动端优先） |\n| 技术栈限制 | 否 | 是否必须使用特定框架或工具 |\n\n---\n\n## 输出期望\n\n| 输出项 | 是否必填 | 说明 |\n|--------|----------|------|\n| ASCII 布局草图 | 是 | 编码前的页面结构示意 |\n| 主题变量定义 | 是 | CSS 自定义属性（颜色、字体、间距、阴影） |\n| 动画规划 | 条件必填 | 如有交互需求，提供微语法描述 |\n| HTML/CSS/JS 代码 | 是 | 完整可运行的前端代码 |\n| 响应式说明 | 是 | 断点设计（手机/平板/桌面） |\n\n---\n\n## 注意事项\n\n- **避免通用蓝色**：不要使用 Bootstrap 风格的 `#007bff`，它会显得过时\n- **优先 oklch()**：现代浏览器支持 `oklch()` 色彩空间，比 `rgb()`/`hsl()` 更均匀\n- **移动端优先**：始终从移动端布局开始，再用 `@media` 扩展到大屏\n- **CDN 仅用于原型**：Tailwind/Flowbite CDN 适合快速原型，生产环境请使用 npm + 构建工具\n- **语义化 HTML**：使用 `<header>`、`<main>`、`<nav>`、`<section>` 等语义标签\n- **对比度达标**：文字与背景对比度至少 4.5:1，确保可读性\n- **不要虚构图片 URL**：使用 Unsplash 或 placehold.co 的真实链接\n- **动画性能**：优先使用 `transform` 和 `opacity` 实现动画，避免触发重排（layout）', observation=<ObservationType.SKILL: 'skill'>, skill='superdesign-frontend', success=True, data={'skill_path':    498 /* 无限滚动行 */
   499 function MarqueeRow({ items, direction = 'left', speed = 28 }: {
   500   items: typeof reviews;
   501   direction?: 'left' | 'right';
   502   speed?: number;
   503 }) {
   504   const doubled = [...items, ...items];
   505   const pausedRef = useRef(false);
   506   const posRef = useRef(0);
   507   const rafRef = useRef(0);
   508   const rowRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   509   const CARD_W = 304; // 288px card + 2*8px mx
   510 
   511   useEffect(() => {
   512     const totalW = items.length * CARD_W;
   513     const step = direction === 'left' ? -speed / 60 : speed / 60;
   514     const animate = () => {
   515       if (!pausedRef.current) {
   516         posRef.current += step;
   517         if (posRef.current <= -totalW) posRef.current += totalW;
   518         if (posRef.current >= 0) posRef.current -= totalW;
   519         if (rowRef.current) rowRef.current.style.transform = `translateX(${posRef.current}px)`;
   520       }
   521       rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
   522     };
   523     rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
   524     return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
   525   }, [direction, speed, items.length]);
   526 
   527   return (
   528     <div
   529       className="overflow-hidden"
   530       onMouseEnter={() => { pausedRef.current = true; }}
   531       onMouseLeave={() => { pausedRef.current = false; }}
   532     >
   533       <div ref={rowRef} className="flex" style={{ willChange: 'transform' }}>
   534         {doubled.map((r, i) => <ReviewCard key={i} review={r} />)}
   535       </div>
   536     </div>
   537   );
   538 }
   539 
   540 function ReviewCarousel() {
   541   const row1 = reviews.slice(0, Math.ceil(reviews.length / 2));
   542   const row2 = reviews.slice(Math.ceil(reviews.length / 2));
   543   // 补足偶数以视觉均衡
   544   const fill = reviews[0];
   545   const r2 = row2.length < 3 ? [...row2, fill, fill] : row2;
   546   return (
   547     <div className="space-y-4 overflow-hidden">
   548       <MarqueeRow items={row1} direction="left" speed={26} />
   549       <MarqueeRow items={r2} direction="right" speed={22} />
   550     </div>
   551   );
   552 }
   553 
   554 /* ─────────────────────── 浮动卡片 3D ─────────────────────── */
   555 function FloatingCard({ children, delay = 0, className = '' }: { children: React.ReactNode; delay?: number; className?: string }) {
   556   return (
   557     <motion.div
   558       initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
   559       whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   560       viewport={{ once: true }}
   561       transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay }}
   562       whileHover={{ y: -6, rotateX: 2, rotateY: -1 }}
   563       style={{ transformStyle: 'preserve-3d' }}
   564       className={className}
   565     >
   566       {children}
   567     </motion.div>
   568   );
   569 }

   366       const orbitNodes: Array<{ mesh: THREE.Mesh; ring: number; angle: number; speed: number }> = [];
   367       ringConfigs.forEach((cfg, ri) => {
   368         const count = 4 + ri * 2;
   369         for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   370           const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.18 + Math.random() * 0.12, 8, 8);
   371           const mat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({ color: cfg.color, emissive: cfg.color, emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.8 : 0.5 });

   378       // ── 鼠标交互 ──
   379       let mouseX = 0, mouseY = 0, targetRotX = 0, targetRotY = 0;
   380       const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
   381         mouseX = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
   382         mouseY = -(e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
   383       };
   384       const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
   385         mouseX = (e.touches[0].clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
   386         mouseY = -(e.touches[0].clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
   387       };
   388       window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
   389       window.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: true });
   390 
   391       const onResize = () => {
   392         camera.aspect = mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight;
   393         camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
   394         renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
   395       };

   399       const clock = new THREE.Clock();
   400       let animId = 0;
   401       const animate = () => {
   402         animId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
   403         const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
   404         targetRotX += (mouseY * 0.4 - targetRotX) * 0.05;
   405         targetRotY += (mouseX * 0.4 - targetRotY) * 0.05;
   406 
   407         core.rotation.x = t * 0.12 + targetRotX;
   408         core.rotation.y = t * 0.18 + targetRotY;
   409         core.rotation.z = t * 0.08;

   411         wireSphere.rotation.y = -t * 0.09 + targetRotY * 0.5;
   412 
   413         rings[0].rotation.y = t * 0.22 + targetRotY;
   414         rings[0].rotation.z = t * 0.08;

   416         rings[1].rotation.z = t * 0.12;

   418         rings[2].rotation.x = -Math.PI / 4 + t * 0.10;

   420         orbitNodes.forEach(node => {
   421           node.angle += node.speed;
   422           const cfg = ringConfigs[node.ring];

   424           localPos.applyEuler(rings[node.ring].rotation);
   425           node.mesh.position.copy(localPos);
   426           node.mesh.scale.setScalar(0.85 + Math.sin(t * 2.5 + node.angle) * 0.15);

   428 
   429         particles.rotation.y = t * 0.025 + targetRotY * 0.15;

   433 
   434         renderer.render(scene, camera);
   435       };
   436       animate();
   437 
   438       cleanup = () => {
   439         cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
   440         window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);

   442         window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
   443         renderer.dispose();

   450       };
   451     });
   452

   461 function ReviewCard({ review }: { review: typeof reviews[0] }) {

   469   ];
   470   const colorIdx = review.name.charCodeAt(0) % avatarColors.length;
   471   return (
   472     <div className="w-72 shrink-0 rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/90 backdrop-blur-sm shadow-md p-5 mx-2 select-none">

   480             <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{review.role}</span>
   481           </div>

New String: ```
import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area';
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { streamChat } from '@/lib/ai';
import {
  Send, Bot, User, Loader2, Square, ImageIcon, Mic, MicOff, Film, X,
  Paperclip, GraduationCap,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

/* ─── Markdown 渲染器（无需第三方库） ─── */
function MarkdownContent({ content, isUser }: { content: string; isUser: boolean }) {
  if (isUser) {
    return <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-pretty">{content}</span>;
  }
  const lines = content.split('\n');
  const elements: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];
    // H1 / H2 / H3
    if (/^### /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h3 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^### /, ''))}</h3>);
    } else if (/^## /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h2 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1.5 text-foreground border-b border-border/40 pb-1">{renderInline(line.replace(/^## /, ''))}</h2>);
    } else if (/^# /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h1 key={i} className="text-base font-extrabold mt-3 mb-2 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^# /, ''))}</h1>);
    // 有序列表
    } else if (/^\d+\. /.test(line.trimStart())) {
      const indent = line.match(/^(\s*)/)?.[1]?.length ?? 0;
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className={`flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed ${indent > 0 ? 'ml-4' : ''}`}>
          <span className="text-primary font-semibold shrink-0 min-w-[1.2rem]">{line.trimStart().match(/^(\d+)\./)?.[1]}.</span>
          <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^\d+\. /, ''))}</span>
        </div>
      );
    // 无序列表 - / * / •
    } else if (/^[-*•] /.test(line.trimStart())) {
      const indent = line.match(/^(\s*)/)?.[1]?.length ?? 0;
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className={`flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed ${indent > 0 ? 'ml-4' : ''}`}>
          <span className="text-primary shrink-0 mt-1">•</span>
          <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^[-*•] /, ''))}</span>
        </div>
      );
    // 代码块开始
    } else if (/^```/.test(line)) {
      const lang = line.replace(/^```/, '').trim();
      const codeLines: string[] = [];
      i++;
      while (i < lines.length && !lines[i].startsWith('```')) {
        codeLines.push(lines[i]);
        i++;
      }
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className="my-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/60">
          {lang && <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-muted/80 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground font-mono border-b border-border/40">{lang}</div>}
          <pre className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed overflow-x-auto bg-muted/50 text-foreground">
            <code>{codeLines.join('\n')}</code>
          </pre>
        </div>
      );
    // 分割线
    } else if (/^---+$/.test(line.trim())) {
      elements.push(<hr key={i} className="my-3 border-border/40" />);
    // 引用块
    } else if (/^> /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(
        <blockquote key={i} className="border-l-2 border-primary/40 pl-3 my-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground italic">
          {renderInline(line.replace(/^> /, ''))}
        </blockquote>
      );
    // 空行
    } else if (line.trim() === '') {
      if (elements.length > 0) elements.push(<div key={i} className="h-1.5" />);
    // 普通段落
    } else {
      elements.push(<p key={i} className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-pretty">{renderInline(line)}</p>);
    }
    i++;
  }
  return <div className="space-y-0.5">{elements}</div>;
}

/* 内联样式：**粗体** `代码` */
function renderInline(text: string): React.ReactNode {
  const parts: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const regex = /(\*\*(.+?)\*\*|`([^`]+)`|\*([^*]+)\*)/g;
  let last = 0;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    if (match.index > last) parts.push(text.slice(last, match.index));
    if (match[2]) parts.push(<strong key={match.index} className="font-semibold text-foreground">{match[2]}</strong>);
    else if (match[3]) parts.push(<code key={match.index} className="px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-muted/70 text-xs font-mono text-primary">{match[3]}</code>);
    else if (match[4]) parts.push(<em key={match.index} className="italic">{match[4]}</em>);
    last = match.index + match[0].length;
  }
  if (last < text.length) parts.push(text.slice(last));
  return parts.length === 1 ? parts[0] : parts;
}

// 资源生成页
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

/* ─── Markdown 块渲染（AI生成内容用） ─── */
function renderMarkdownBlocks(text: string): React.ReactNode {
  const lines = text.split('\n');
  const elements: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];
    if (/^### /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h3 key={i} className="text-sm font-bold mt-3 mb-1 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^### /, ''))}</h3>);
    } else if (/^## /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h2 key={i} className="text-sm font-semibold mt-3 mb-1.5 text-primary border-b border-border/40 pb-1">{renderInline(line.replace(/^## /, ''))}</h2>);
    } else if (/^# /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<h1 key={i} className="text-base font-bold mt-3 mb-2 text-foreground">{renderInline(line.replace(/^# /, ''))}</h1>);
    } else if (/^\d+\. /.test(line.trimStart())) {
      const num = line.trimStart().match(/^(\d+)\./)?.[1];
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className="flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed">
          <span className="text-primary font-semibold shrink-0 min-w-[1.2rem]">{num}.</span>
          <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^\d+\. /, ''))}</span>
        </div>
      );
    } else if (/^[-*•] /.test(line.trimStart())) {
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className="flex gap-2 text-sm leading-relaxed">
          <span className="text-primary shrink-0 mt-1">•</span>
          <span className="text-pretty">{renderInline(line.trimStart().replace(/^[-*•] /, ''))}</span>
        </div>
      );
    } else if (/^```/.test(line)) {
      const lang = line.replace(/^```/, '').trim();
      const codeLines: string[] = [];
      i++;
      while (i < lines.length && !lines[i].startsWith('```')) { codeLines.push(lines[i]); i++; }
      elements.push(
        <div key={i} className="my-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-border/60">
          {lang && <div className="px-3 py-1 bg-muted text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground border-b border-border/40">{lang}</div>}
          <pre className="px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed overflow-x-auto bg-muted/50 text-foreground"><code>{codeLines.join('\n')}</code></pre>
        </div>
      );
    } else if (/^---+$/.test(line.trim())) {
      elements.push(<hr key={i} className="my-3 border-border/40" />);
    } else if (/^> /.test(line)) {
      elements.push(<blockquote key={i} className="border-l-2 border-primary/50 pl-3 my-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground italic">{renderInline(line.replace(/^> /, ''))}</blockquote>);
    } else if (line.trim() === '') {
      if (elements.length > 0) elements.push(<div key={i} className="h-1.5" />);
    } else {
      elements.push(<p key={i} className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-pretty">{renderInline(line)}</p>);
    }
    i++;
  }
  return <div className="space-y-0.5 px-1">{elements}</div>;
}

function renderInline(text: string): React.ReactNode {
  const parts: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const regex = /(\*\*(.+?)\*\*|`([^`]+)`|\*([^*]+)\*)/g;
  let last = 0; let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
    if (match.index > last) parts.push(text.slice(last, match.index));
    if (match[2]) parts.push(<strong key={match.index} className="font-semibold text-foreground">{match[2]}</strong>);
    else if (match[3]) parts.push(<code key={match.index} className="px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-muted text-xs font-mono text-primary">{match[3]}</code>);
    else if (match[4]) parts.push(<em key={match.index}>{match[4]}</em>);
    last = match.index + match[0].length;
  }
  if (last < text.length) parts.push(text.slice(last));
  return parts.length === 1 ? parts[0] : parts;
}

/* ─────────────────────── Three.js 3D Hero 背景 ─────────────────────── */
function HeroBackground() {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;
    let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;

    import('three').then((THREE) => {
      if (!mountRef.current) return;

      // ── 场景、相机、渲染器 ──
      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 200);
      camera.position.set(0, 0, 28);

      const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
      renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
      renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
      renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
      renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
      renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;
      mount.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

      const PRIMARY_H = 162;
      const primaryColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H}, 63%, 45%)`);
      const accentColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H + 40}, 70%, 55%)`);
      const deepColor = new THREE.Color(`hsl(220, 70%, 55%)`);

      // ── 雾气 & 背景 ──
      scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(isDark ? 0x040b10 : 0xeef7f4, 0.018);
      scene.background = new THREE.Color(isDark ? 0x040b10 : 0xeef7f4);

      // ── 光照 ──

      const dirLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(primaryColor, isDark ? 1.8 : 1.2);
      dirLight1.position.set(10, 15, 10);
      scene.add(dirLight1);
      const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(deepColor, isDark ? 1.0 : 0.6);
      dirLight2.position.set(-10, -8, 5);
      scene.add(dirLight2);
      const pointLight = new THREE.PointLight(accentColor, isDark ? 2.5 : 1.5, 40);
      pointLight.position.set(0, 0, 15);
      scene.add(pointLight);

      // ── 粒子群 ──
      const particleCount = 1800;
      const particleGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
      const pColors = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
      const sizes = new Float32Array(particleCount);

        new THREE.Color(`hsl(${PRIMARY_H + 20}, 80%, 65%)`),
        new THREE.Color('hsl(200, 80%, 60%)'),
      ];

        const r = 18 + Math.random() * 20;
        const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
        positions[i * 3]     = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
        positions[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);
        positions[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.cos(phi);
        const c = colorOptions[Math.floor(Math.random() * colorOptions.length)];
        pColors[i * 3]     = c.r;
        pColors[i * 3 + 1] = c.g;
        pColors[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;
        sizes[i] = Math.random() * 2.5 + 0.5;
      }
      particleGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
      particleGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pColors, 3));
      particleGeo.setAttribute('size', new THREE.BufferAttribute(sizes, 1));

      const particleMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        vertexShader: `
          attribute float size;
          attribute vec3 color;
          varying vec3 vColor;
          varying float vAlpha;
          void main() {
            vColor = color;
            vec4 mvPos = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
            float depth = (-mvPos.z - 10.0) / 50.0;
            vAlpha = clamp(1.0 - depth, 0.15, 0.9);
            gl_PointSize = size * (300.0 / -mvPos.z);
            gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvPos;
          }
        `,
        fragmentShader: `
          varying vec3 vColor;
          varying float vAlpha;
          void main() {
            float d = length(gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5));
            if (d > 0.5) discard;
            float alpha = smoothstep(0.5, 0.0, d) * vAlpha;
            gl_FragColor = vec4(vColor, alpha);
          }
        `,
        transparent: true,
        depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        vertexColors: true,
      });
      const particles = new THREE.Points(particleGeo, particleMat);
      scene.add(particles);

      // ── 中心能量球 ──
      const coreGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(3.5, 4);
      const coreMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
        color: primaryColor,
        metalness: 0.3,
        roughness: 0.1,
        transmission: 0.6,
        thickness: 1.5,
        envMapIntensity: 2.0,
        emissive: primaryColor,
        emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.3 : 0.15,
      });
      const core = new THREE.Mesh(coreGeo, coreMat);
      scene.add(core);

      // ── 外层线框球 ──
      const wireGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(4.6, 2);
      const wireMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
        color: primaryColor, wireframe: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: isDark ? 0.12 : 0.08,
      });
      const wireSphere = new THREE.Mesh(wireGeo, wireMat);
      scene.add(wireSphere);

      // ── 轨道环 ──
      const rings: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
      const ringConfigs = [
        { r: 7.5,  tube: 0.03,  color: primaryColor, opacity: isDark ? 0.5 : 0.35,  rotX: Math.PI / 3, rotY: 0 },
        { r: 10,   tube: 0.025, color: accentColor,   opacity: isDark ? 0.35 : 0.25, rotX: Math.PI / 5, rotY: Math.PI / 4 },
        { r: 13,   tube: 0.02,  color: deepColor,     opacity: isDark ? 0.25 : 0.18, rotX: -Math.PI / 4, rotY: Math.PI / 6 },
      ];
      ringConfigs.forEach(cfg => {
        const geo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(cfg.r, cfg.tube, 16, 120);
        const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: cfg.color, transparent: true, opacity: cfg.opacity });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
        mesh.rotation.x = cfg.rotX;
        mesh.rotation.y = cfg.rotY;
        rings.push(mesh);
        scene.add(mesh);
      });

      // ── 轨道节点 ──
      const orbitNodes: Array<{ mesh: THREE.Mesh; ring: number; angle: number; speed: number }> = [];
      ringConfigs.forEach((cfg, ri) => {
        const count = 4 + ri * 2;
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.18 + Math.random() * 0.12, 8, 8);
          const mat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({ color: cfg.color, emissive: cfg.color, emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.8 : 0.5 });
          const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
          orbitNodes.push({ mesh, ring: ri, angle: (i / count) * Math.PI * 2, speed: 0.004 + Math.random() * 0.003 });
          scene.add(mesh);
        }
      });

      // ── 鼠标交互 ──
      let mouseX = 0, mouseY = 0, targetRotX = 0, targetRotY = 0;
      const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
        mouseX = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
        mouseY = -(e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
      };
      const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
        mouseX = (e.touches[0].clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
        mouseY = -(e.touches[0].clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
      };
      window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      window.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: true });

      const onResize = () => {
        camera.aspect = mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
      };
      window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);

      // ── 渲染循环 ──
      const clock = new THREE.Clock();
      let animId = 0;
      const animate = () => {
        animId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        targetRotX += (mouseY * 0.4 - targetRotX) * 0.05;
        targetRotY += (mouseX * 0.4 - targetRotY) * 0.05;

        core.rotation.x = t * 0.12 + targetRotX;
        core.rotation.y = t * 0.18 + targetRotY;
        core.rotation.z = t * 0.08;
        wireSphere.rotation.x = -t * 0.06 + targetRotX * 0.5;
        wireSphere.rotation.y = -t * 0.09 + targetRotY * 0.5;

        rings[0].rotation.y = t * 0.22 + targetRotY;
        rings[0].rotation.z = t * 0.08;
        rings[1].rotation.x = Math.PI / 5 + t * 0.15 + targetRotX * 0.6;
        rings[1].rotation.z = t * 0.12;
        rings[2].rotation.y = -t * 0.18 + targetRotY * 0.4;
        rings[2].rotation.x = -Math.PI / 4 + t * 0.10;

        orbitNodes.forEach(node => {
          node.angle += node.speed;
          const cfg = ringConfigs[node.ring];
          const localPos = new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(node.angle) * cfg.r, 0, Math.sin(node.angle) * cfg.r);
          localPos.applyEuler(rings[node.ring].rotation);
          node.mesh.position.copy(localPos);
          node.mesh.scale.setScalar(0.85 + Math.sin(t * 2.5 + node.angle) * 0.15);
        });

        particles.rotation.y = t * 0.025 + targetRotY * 0.15;
        particles.rotation.x = t * 0.015 + targetRotX * 0.15;
        pointLight.intensity = (isDark ? 2.5 : 1.5) + Math.sin(t * 1.8) * 0.6;
        pointLight.position.set(Math.sin(t * 0.5) * 8, Math.cos(t * 0.4) * 5, 15 + Math.sin(t * 0.7) * 3);

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
      };
      animate();

      cleanup = () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
        window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
        window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
        window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
        renderer.dispose();
        particleGeo.dispose(); particleMat.dispose();
        coreGeo.dispose(); coreMat.dispose();
        wireGeo.dispose(); wireMat.dispose();
        rings.forEach(r => { r.geometry.dispose(); (r.material as THREE.Material).dispose(); });
        orbitNodes.forEach(n => { n.mesh.geometry.dispose(); (n.mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose(); });
        if (mount.contains(renderer.domElement)) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
      };
    });

    return () => { cleanup?.(); };
  }, []);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full" style={{ zIndex: 0 }} />;
}

/* ─────────────────────── Three.js 神经网络 Hero 背景 ─────────────────────── */
function HeroBackground() {
  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const mount = mountRef.current;
    if (!mount) return;
    let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;

    import('three').then((THREE) => {
      if (!mountRef.current) return;

      const W = mount.clientWidth, H = mount.clientHeight;
      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(55, W / H, 0.1, 300);
      camera.position.set(0, 0, 40);

      const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });
      renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
      renderer.setSize(W, H);
      renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
      renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
      renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.3;
      mount.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

      const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');

      // ── 背景色 ──
      scene.background = new THREE.Color(isDark ? 0x030a0f : 0xf0f8f5);
      scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(isDark ? 0x030a0f : 0xf0f8f5, 50, 120);

      // ── 颜色系统 ──
      const C = {
        primary:  new THREE.Color('hsl(162,63%,45%)'),
        accent1:  new THREE.Color('hsl(200,75%,55%)'),
        accent2:  new THREE.Color('hsl(130,60%,50%)'),
        accent3:  new THREE.Color('hsl(270,65%,60%)'),
        accent4:  new THREE.Color('hsl(40,80%,55%)'),
      };

      // ── 神经网络节点 ──
      const NODE_COUNT = 80;
      interface NNode { pos: THREE.Vector3; vel: THREE.Vector3; mesh: THREE.Mesh; color: THREE.Color; phase: number }
      const palette = [C.primary, C.accent1, C.accent2, C.accent3, C.accent4];
      const nodes: NNode[] = [];
      const nodeGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 12, 12);
      for (let i = 0; i < NODE_COUNT; i++) {
        const color = palette[i % palette.length];
        const mat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
          color,
          emissive: color,
          emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.7 : 0.3,
          shininess: 120,
        });
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(nodeGeo, mat);
        const spread = 28;
        mesh.position.set(
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread,
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.65,
          (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.4,
        );
        const speed = 0.012 + Math.random() * 0.018;
        nodes.push({
          pos: mesh.position.clone(),
          vel: new THREE.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed * 0.5,
          ),
          mesh,
          color,
          phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
        });
        scene.add(mesh);
      }

      // ── 连接线（用 LineSegments 一次性绘制） ──
      const MAX_DIST = 9.5;
      const linePositions = new Float32Array(NODE_COUNT * NODE_COUNT * 6);
      const lineColors = new Float32Array(NODE_COUNT * NODE_COUNT * 6);
      const lineGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      const linePosAttr = new THREE.BufferAttribute(linePositions, 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      const lineColAttr = new THREE.BufferAttribute(lineColors, 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      lineGeo.setAttribute('position', linePosAttr);
      lineGeo.setAttribute('color', lineColAttr);
      const lineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
        vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true,
        opacity: isDark ? 0.45 : 0.3,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
        depthWrite: false,
      });
      const lineSegments = new THREE.LineSegments(lineGeo, lineMat);
      scene.add(lineSegments);

      // ── 浮动粒子云 ──
      const PART = 1400;
      const pGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      const pPos = new Float32Array(PART * 3);
      const pCol = new Float32Array(PART * 3);
      for (let i = 0; i < PART; i++) {
        const r = 22 + Math.random() * 18;
        const t = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const p = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
        pPos[i * 3]     = r * Math.sin(p) * Math.cos(t);
        pPos[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.sin(p) * Math.sin(t);
        pPos[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.cos(p);
        const c = palette[i % palette.length];
        pCol[i * 3] = c.r; pCol[i * 3 + 1] = c.g; pCol[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;
      }
      pGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pPos, 3));
      pGeo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pCol, 3));
      const pMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
        vertexShader: `
          attribute vec3 color; varying vec3 vColor; varying float vA;
          void main() {
            vColor = color;
            vec4 mvp = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
            vA = clamp(1.0 - (-mvp.z - 8.0) / 55.0, 0.05, 0.7);
            gl_PointSize = 1.8 * (280.0 / -mvp.z);
            gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mvp;
          }
        `,
        fragmentShader: `
          varying vec3 vColor; varying float vA;
          void main() {
            float d = length(gl_PointCoord - 0.5);
            if (d > 0.5) discard;
            gl_FragColor = vec4(vColor, smoothstep(0.5, 0.0, d) * vA);
          }
        `,
        transparent: true, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, vertexColors: true,
      });
      const particles = new THREE.Points(pGeo, pMat);
      scene.add(particles);

      // ── DNA 双螺旋 (中心左侧偏移) ──
      const helixNodes: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
      const helixLines: THREE.Line[] = [];
      const HELIX_SEG = 32;
      const helixGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.15, 8, 8);
      for (let i = 0; i < HELIX_SEG; i++) {
        const t = i / HELIX_SEG;
        const angle = t * Math.PI * 4;
        const y = (t - 0.5) * 18;
        const r = 2.2;
        const c1 = i % 2 === 0 ? C.primary : C.accent1;
        const c2 = i % 2 === 0 ? C.accent3 : C.accent2;
        [c1, c2].forEach((col, si) => {
          const sign = si === 0 ? 1 : -1;
          const mat = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({ color: col, emissive: col, emissiveIntensity: isDark ? 0.6 : 0.25 });
          const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(helixGeo, mat);
          mesh.position.set(sign * r * Math.cos(angle) - 12, y, sign * r * Math.sin(angle) - 5);
          helixNodes.push(mesh);
          scene.add(mesh);
        });
        // 横向连接杆
        if (i < HELIX_SEG - 1) {
          const pts = [
            new THREE.Vector3(r * Math.cos(angle) - 12, y, r * Math.sin(angle) - 5),
            new THREE.Vector3(-r * Math.cos(angle) - 12, y, -r * Math.sin(angle) - 5),
          ];
          const lGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts);
          const lMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: C.accent1, transparent: true, opacity: isDark ? 0.25 : 0.15 });
          const line = new THREE.Line(lGeo, lMat);
          helixLines.push(line);
          scene.add(line);
        }
      }

      // ── 光照 ──
      scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, isDark ? 0.3 : 0.6));
      const dLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(C.primary, isDark ? 1.5 : 0.8);
      dLight.position.set(12, 18, 12); scene.add(dLight);
      const pLight1 = new THREE.PointLight(C.accent3, isDark ? 2.0 : 1.0, 50);
      pLight1.position.set(-15, 5, 10); scene.add(pLight1);
      const pLight2 = new THREE.PointLight(C.accent1, isDark ? 1.8 : 0.9, 45);
      pLight2.position.set(15, -5, 8); scene.add(pLight2);

      // ── 鼠标 ──
      let mX = 0, mY = 0, tRX = 0, tRY = 0;
      const onMM = (e: MouseEvent) => { mX = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2; mY = -(e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2; };
      const onTM = (e: TouchEvent) => { mX = (e.touches[0].clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2; mY = -(e.touches[0].clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2; };
      window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMM);
      window.addEventListener('touchmove', onTM, { passive: true });
      const onResize = () => {
        camera.aspect = mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(mount.clientWidth, mount.clientHeight);
      };
      window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);

      const clock = new THREE.Clock();
      const tmpColor = new THREE.Color();
      let animId = 0;
      const animate = () => {
        animId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        tRX += (mY * 0.25 - tRX) * 0.04;
        tRY += (mX * 0.25 - tRY) * 0.04;

        // 节点移动 & 边界反弹
        const BOUND = 14;
        nodes.forEach(n => {
          n.mesh.position.addScaledVector(n.vel, 1);
          if (Math.abs(n.mesh.position.x) > BOUND) n.vel.x *= -1;
          if (Math.abs(n.mesh.position.y) > BOUND * 0.65) n.vel.y *= -1;
          if (Math.abs(n.mesh.position.z) > BOUND * 0.35) n.vel.z *= -1;
          const pulse = 0.85 + Math.sin(t * 2.2 + n.phase) * 0.15;
          n.mesh.scale.setScalar(pulse);
          (n.mesh.material as THREE.MeshPhongMaterial).emissiveIntensity = (isDark ? 0.5 : 0.2) * pulse;
        });

        // 更新连接线
        let li = 0;
        for (let a = 0; a < NODE_COUNT; a++) {
          for (let b = a + 1; b < NODE_COUNT; b++) {
            const dist = nodes[a].mesh.position.distanceTo(nodes[b].mesh.position);
            if (dist < MAX_DIST) {
              const alpha = 1 - dist / MAX_DIST;
              const base = li * 6;
              linePositions[base]     = nodes[a].mesh.position.x;
              linePositions[base + 1] = nodes[a].mesh.position.y;
              linePositions[base + 2] = nodes[a].mesh.position.z;
              linePositions[base + 3] = nodes[b].mesh.position.x;
              linePositions[base + 4] = nodes[b].mesh.position.y;
              linePositions[base + 5] = nodes[b].mesh.position.z;
              tmpColor.lerpColors(nodes[a].color, nodes[b].color, 0.5);
              lineColors[base]     = tmpColor.r * alpha;
              lineColors[base + 1] = tmpColor.g * alpha;
              lineColors[base + 2] = tmpColor.b * alpha;
              lineColors[base + 3] = tmpColor.r * alpha;
              lineColors[base + 4] = tmpColor.g * alpha;
              lineColors[base + 5] = tmpColor.b * alpha;
              li++;
            }
          }
        }
        lineGeo.setDrawRange(0, li * 2);
        linePosAttr.needsUpdate = true;
        lineColAttr.needsUpdate = true;

        // 粒子 & DNA 整体微旋
        particles.rotation.y = t * 0.018 + tRY * 0.12;
        particles.rotation.x = t * 0.010 + tRX * 0.12;
        const helixGroup = { x: t * 0.12, y: t * 0.06 };
        helixNodes.forEach(n => { n.rotation.y = helixGroup.y; });
        helixLines.forEach(l => { l.rotation.y = helixGroup.y; });

        // 整场景微摆
        scene.rotation.y = tRY * 0.08;
        scene.rotation.x = tRX * 0.05;

        // 脉冲灯
        pLight1.intensity = (isDark ? 2.0 : 1.0) + Math.sin(t * 1.4) * 0.5;
        pLight2.intensity = (isDark ? 1.8 : 0.9) + Math.cos(t * 1.1) * 0.4;

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
      };
      animate();

      cleanup = () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animId);
        window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMM);
        window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTM);
        window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
        renderer.dispose();
        nodeGeo.dispose(); lineGeo.dispose(); lineMat.dispose(); pGeo.dispose(); pMat.dispose(); helixGeo.dispose();
        nodes.forEach(n => (n.mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose());
        helixNodes.forEach(n => (n.material as THREE.Material).dispose());
        helixLines.forEach(l => { l.geometry.dispose(); (l.material as THREE.Material).dispose(); });
        if (mount.contains(renderer.domElement)) mount.removeChild(renderer.domElement);
      };
    });

    return () => { cleanup?.(); };
  }, []);

  return <div ref={mountRef} className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full" style={{ zIndex: 0 }} />;
}

```

   522               <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl bg-muted flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-5">
   523                 <Route className="w-10 h-10 text-muted-foreground/30" />
   524               </div>
   525               <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">选择上方资源类型，一键生成学习路径</h3>
   526               <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground max-w-sm text-pretty">
   527                 系统将结合你的学习画像与所选资源类型，为你规划科学的个性化学习步骤
   528               </p>
   529             </div>
   530           </div>
   531         ) : (
   532           <div
   533             className="flex-1 min-h-0 rounded-2xl border border-border overflow-auto"
   534             style={{
   535               background: 'radial-gradient(hsl(var(--border) / 0.6) 1px, transparent 1px)',
   536               backgroundSize: '24px 24px',
   537               minHeight: '360px',
   538             }}
   539           >
   540             <div
   541               className="relative p-8"
   542               style={{
   543                 transform: `scale(${zoom})`,
   544                 transformOrigin: 'top left',
   545                 minWidth: `${path.stages.length * 288 + 128}px`,
   546                 minHeight: '340px',
   547               }}
   548             >
   549               {/* SVG 连线层 */}
   550               <svg
   551                 className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
   552                 style={{ width: `${path.stages.length * 288 + 128}px`, height: '340px' }}
   553               >
   554                 <defs>
   555                   <marker id="arrowDone" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto">
   556                     <path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="hsl(var(--primary))" />
   557                   </marker>
   558                   <marker id="arrowTodo" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="7" refY="3" orient="auto">
   559                     <path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="hsl(var(--border))" />
   560                   </marker>
   561                 </defs>
   562                 {path.stages.map((stage, i) => {
   563                   if (i >= path.stages.length - 1) return null;
   564                   const x1 = i * 288 + 256 + 16;
   565                   const x2 = (i + 1) * 288 + 16;
   566                   const y = 108;
   567                   return (
   568                     <g key={`edge-${stage.id}`}>
   569                       <line x1={x1} y1={y} x2={x2} y2={y}
   570                         stroke={stage.completed ? 'hsl(var(--primary))' : 'hsl(var(--border))'}
   571                         strokeWidth="2.5"
   572                         strokeDasharray={stage.completed ? '0' : '7 5'}
   573                         strokeLinecap="round"
   574                         markerEnd={stage.completed ? 'url(#arrowDone)' : 'url(#arrowTodo)'}
   575                       />
   576                       {stage.completed && (
   577                         <circle r="4" fill="hsl(var(--primary))" opacity="0.9">
   578                           <animateMotion dur="1.6s" repeatCount="indefinite"
   579                             path={`M${x1},${y} L${x2},${y}`} />
   580                         </circle>
   581                       )}
   582                     </g>
   583                   );
   584                 })}
   585               </svg>
   586 
   587               {/* 节点行 */}
   588               <div className="flex items-start gap-8 relative z-10">
   589                 {path.stages.map((stage, index) => {
   590                   const isCurrent = index === path.current_stage && !stage.completed;
   591                   return (
   592                     <StageNode
   593                       key={stage.id}
   594                       stage={stage}
   595                       index={index}
   596                       isCurrent={isCurrent}
   597                       isCompleted={stage.completed}
   598                       isLocked={!stage.completed && !isCurrent}
   599                       onComplete={() => completeStage(stage.id)}
   600                       completing={completingId === stage.id}
   601                       onDelete={() => deleteStage(stage.id)}
   602                       onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; setDragIndex(index); }}
   603                       onDragOver={e => { e.preventDefault(); e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move'; }}
   604                       onDrop={e => { e.preventDefault(); handleDrop(index); }}
   605                     />
   606                   );
   607                 })}
   608 
   609                 {/* ── 新增节点按钮/表单 ── */}
   610                 <div className="shrink-0" style={{ width: 256 }}>
   611                   <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
   612                     {addingNode ? (
   613                       <motion.div key="form" initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
   614                         exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
   615                         className="bg-card rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-primary/40 p-4 space-y-3 shadow-sm"
   616                         style={{ marginTop: 6 }}
   617                       >
   618                         <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5">
   619                           <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />新增学习节点
   620                         </p>
   621                         <input
   622                           ref={addInputRef}
   623                           value={newNodeTitle}
   624                           onChange={e => setNewNodeTitle(e.target.value)}
   625                           placeholder="节点名称（必填）"
   626                           className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:border-primary placeholder:text-muted-foreground/50"
   627                         />
   628                         <textarea
   629                           value={newNodeDesc}
   630                           onChange={e => setNewNodeDesc(e.target.value)}
   631                           placeholder="描述（可选）"
   632                           rows={2}
   633                           className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:border-primary resize-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/50"
   634                         />
   635                         <div className="flex gap-2">
   636                           <Button size="sm" className="flex-1 h-7 text-xs" onClick={addStage} disabled={!newNodeTitle.trim()}>确认</Button>
   637                           <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="h-7 text-xs" onClick={() => { setAddingNode(false); setNewNodeTitle(''); setNewNodeDesc(''); }}>取消</Button>
   638                         </div>
   639                       </motion.div>
   640                     ) : (
   641                       <motion.button key="btn" type="button" initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
   642                         onClick={() => setAddingNode(true)}
   643                         className="w-full h-28 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-border hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-primary/5 transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 group"
   644                         style={{ marginTop: 6 }}
   645                       >
   646                         <Plus className="w-6 h-6 text-muted-foreground/40 group-hover:text-primary transition-colors" />
   647                         <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground/60 group-hover:text-primary transition-colors">新增节点</span>
   648                       </motion.button>
   649                     )}
   650                   </AnimatePresence>
   651                 </div>
   652               </div>
   653 
   654               {/* 图例 */}
   655               <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-4 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground/70">
   656                 <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><span className="w-3 h-0.5 bg-primary rounded-full" />已完成</div>
